# Not verified on screen, item by item

What each finished item did NOT confirm in the running game or in the original, verbatim from `../STATUS.md`,
newest first. The pre-commit hook (`tools/status-sweep.py`) moves an item's account here when the next item's is
written above it. **Grep it by Q-number; never read it whole.** Each account is frozen at its item's date: a
later item may have run what an earlier one lists as untested.

- Q652 (the terrain model's meshes half lit outside the park, the water unlit): this game on the desk at 1280 x 720
  (`q652/run1`, the census line `9307 vertices of 271 terrain meshes made 0.225 long`), main as the before
  (`q652/run0-main`), the original off-screen (`q652/orig`), both at (500,500) and (700,640) 130 rot 90. Not seen in
  the running game: a face turned from the sun on half-lit land (none at these cameras), the other three themes, the
  lobby's and the rides' unlit materials (Q401 confirms a sign), and what the original draws for its NaN normals (the
  bridge's ends, 64 of 80 vertices; this game lights its own normal). The bridge's waterfall curtain reads brighter
  here than the original's, in main too (filed under Q401).
- Q649 (a built thing's floor blended into the ground's tiles): this game on the desk at 1280 x 720 (`q649/run2`, the
  final build), the original off-screen at 32-pixel tiles (`q649/orig`), both at the Belly Bounce (525,240) 110 180.
  Not seen in the running game: a thing turned 180 or 270 bigger than one cell (none stands so in the stock park; the
  stamp's walk is the listing's, `ProceduralFloorTests`), the faithful 16-pixel tiles, and the other three themes.
  The bought Drinks Shop was seen in this game alone, not beside the original's.
- Q656 (a fresh park's boundary wall): this game on the desk at 1280 x 720 (`q656/run1`), the original off-screen by
  `freshfs.sh` (`q656/orig`), both at the cameras (450,165) and (600,100), 110, 180. Not seen: the left of the gate in the
  original (its 4:3 frame cuts it off at that camera; its file holds it). Not built: how often the original's laying
  draws the generator before each anchor's last choice (`FRESH_BOUNDARY_LAYING_DRAWS`). Not run: fantasy, Hallowe'en
  and space fresh parks in this game (Lost Kingdom first; the chooser matches the original's fantasy and Hallowe'en files).
- Q743 (the original on Q713's made file B, row 248): the original run off-screen under Proton by `fsload.sh`, its
  memory read and its frames taken (`q743/orig/O`); this game on the desk at 1280 x 720, Full Simulation by Q707's F player.
  Not read: the original's score for row 248 (posted and taken on one tick), which sideshow its row 257 judged at 88
  (Q662). Not run: Instant Action (rows 238 to 257 read no mode), file D in the original (Q662). Not heard (dummy audio).

- Q723 (the sample draw; lane B, off-screen): textures are drawn white there, so the claims rest on the log and the census, not on a picture (`q723/run3/lobby.png`, `park.png` are named, not evidence). The lobby's music never repeats a track in the attract lobby's four islands (ten to twelve selections each in 240 s, `q723/run3/run.log`); the later samples of the park's plain-class and music-class voices (the crowd, the land's following voices, the park music, a script's plain and music sounds, the interface's loops) ask for the step in the original and are counted `SAMPLE_NO_REPEAT`, not built (Q742); a voice's first sample does not ask and is not counted. The no-sample case (a roll above a list's last odds, at most 20 in 65,536) is unit-tested, never seen in a run. Not heard: the mix was not captured, so which track sounded is the log's word. The original was not run in this lane. Full Simulation not run: the draw is the same code. The test that failed once in a full run was most likely `SoundCategoryTests.TheGlobalRidesPlayTheSamplesTheirRecordsName` (a roll above 202's last odds, 1.8% a run), found by the run 9 review and fixed with a seam for the category's roll.
